# TriggerTrade — Retained 60-Candidate Pool — Corrected R3

**OPERATIVE_R3 — `RESEARCH_V1_FOCUSED_CORRECTION_R3`. Status: APPROVED — specification-level package correction only.**

Exactly the two approved assignments are applied: **R-022 = C-012, BTC contextual agreement (AX-08)**; **R-026 = C-015, Local-momentum agreement (AX-11)**. The other 28 questions, treatments, Set mappings, Position/Portfolio configurations and BTC applicability are unchanged. R-023 remains C-036; R-010 retains its unchanged definition and conditional first-wave economic-audit qualification.

Current counts: **30 valid hypotheses; 13 Set / 11 Position / 4 Portfolio / 2 cross-layer**. Sixty candidates remain. Ten fixed 10% allocations total exactly 100%; R-006 BTC remains NOT_APPLICABLE without redistribution. The frozen v1.2.15 archive SHA-256 is `3b38c09c6f52daa77d7cc4257a3c420b21d76ac10d039dd708dd205af963b326` and its bytes are unchanged.

Package/research identity is R3. Existing immutable execution-semantic pins (`profile_revision=RESEARCH_V1_FOCUSED_CORRECTION_R2`), `REPORT-R-V1-R2`, `EVIDENCE-R-V1-R2`, and `DEC-R-001`/R2 deliberately remain unchanged contracts. They do **not** select an R2 hypothesis: launch always resolves the R3 research revision separately. Existing Set/Trigger/configuration revisions and digests are not relabeled. The current profile document explains this compatibility explicitly. No old/new hypothesis runs are pooled.

Authority: frozen methodology; unchanged R2 package; focused BTC/intrabar council; R2 economic and pairwise audits; approved replacement selection and pairwise review; current mechanical-correction instruction. Only `operative/` is current configuration. `provenance/` is HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE. No Backtest, Demo, native API, data availability, statistical independence or profitability is certified.

## Mechanical selection application

C-012 and C-015 are now FINAL_SELECTED at R-022 and R-026 respectively. Their original questions/axes/predicates and non-BTC contrasts are reused; the two BTC variants are materialized in the normalized registry. C-049 and C-053 are source-valid historical/conditional RESERVE entries, not invalid mechanisms and not current finals. No C-061/C-062 exists. C-048, C-056 and all unrelated reserve/excluded/eliminated definitions are unchanged. R2 selection history, including C-036 replacing the invalid allocation candidate, is preserved in `../provenance/r2_operative/`.

Selected candidates now have YES specification-level executability because the previously missing BTC definitions and dependency bindings exist. This is not empirical validation. Original R2 bodies for all60 are preserved byte-for-byte in provenance; fifty-six current body blocks are also retained byte-for-byte here.

```yaml
  selection?
```

## C-010 — Raw relative-return agreement

```yaml
CANDIDATE_ID: C-010
TITLE: Raw relative-return agreement
PRIMARY_AXIS: AX-06
HYPOTHESIS_CLASS: SET_LAYER
PRIMARY_RESEARCH_QUESTION: Does requiring raw 15m asset-versus-BTC relative return to agree with direction improve
  selection?
PRIMARY_CHANGED_VARIABLE: 'Side-aligned RELATIVE_RETURN_15m sign confirmation: absent -> required'
EXPECTED_INFORMATION_GAIN: Relative performance agreement, not retuning the z-score veto.
SELECTION_DISPOSITION: FINAL_SELECTED
DEPENDENCY_RISK: CONDITIONALLY_INDEPENDENT
ELIMINATION_OR_RESERVE_REASON: ''
BASELINE:
  set: SET-R-001-V1
  position: POS-R-001
  portfolio: PR-R-201
  set_reference_scope: NON_BTC_DEFAULT_ONLY; explicit set_by_symbol is authoritative for execution
  set_by_symbol:
    BTC: null
    ETH: SET-R-001-V1
    SOL: SET-R-001-V1
    XRP: SET-R-001-V1
    DOGE: SET-R-001-V1
    SUI: SET-R-001-V1
    PEPE: SET-R-001-V1
    AVAX: SET-R-001-V1
    LINK: SET-R-001-V1
    BNB: SET-R-001-V1
  symbol_applicability:
    BTC:
      status: NOT_APPLICABLE
      reason: SOURCE_LOGIC_NOT_MEANINGFUL_FOR_BTC
      allocation_pct: '10'
      redistribution: false
VARIANT:

  position: POS-R-001

  profiles and evidence-retention controls to all comparable arms. All ten allocations are exactly 10%, sum 100%;
```

## C-011 — Aggressive-flow agreement

```yaml

  selection?
```

## C-015 — Local-momentum agreement

```yaml
CANDIDATE_ID: C-015
TITLE: Local-momentum agreement
PRIMARY_AXIS: AX-11
HYPOTHESIS_CLASS: SET_LAYER
PRIMARY_RESEARCH_QUESTION: Does requiring the 5m local VNM z-score to agree with direction improve selection?
PRIMARY_CHANGED_VARIABLE: Additional same-side VNM_5m_z sign confirmation
EXPECTED_INFORMATION_GAIN: Local momentum agreement rather than absence of opposite veto.
SELECTION_DISPOSITION: FINAL_SELECTED
DEPENDENCY_RISK: CONDITIONALLY_INDEPENDENT
```

```yaml
  current qualification?
```

## C-020 — Direction maintained through formation

```yaml
  sequence improve selection?
```

## C-021 — Participation conjunction versus disjunction

```yaml

PRIMARY_CHANGED_VARIABLE: 'Participation composition: AND -> OR with identical member predicates'
EXPECTED_INFORMATION_GAIN: Substitutability versus complementarity of two different participation measures.

    - tranche_open_notional_at_funding_time
    - unrealized_pnl
    direction_authority: DB-R-BTC-001
    classifier_object_produced: false
    dependency_rule: Only canonical dependencies of actually bound BTC predicates, pending condition and reached
      downstream stages. No F-004 aggregate/relative normalization/F-005 gate/veto/diagnostic is required or fabricated;
      full union list is not a universal prerequisite.
```

## C-024 — Alternative re-arm policy

```yaml
CANDIDATE_ID: C-024
TITLE: Alternative re-arm policy
PRIMARY_AXIS: AX-20
HYPOTHESIS_CLASS: SET_LAYER
PRIMARY_RESEARCH_QUESTION: Does AFTER_RESET differ from AFTER_ALL_REQUIRED_TRIGGERS_CLEAR under an explicitly supplied
  reset protocol?
```

## C-035 — Dynamic Stop buffer

```yaml
CANDIDATE_ID: C-035
TITLE: Dynamic Stop buffer
PRIMARY_AXIS: AX-26
HYPOTHESIS_CLASS: POSITION_RULES
PRIMARY_RESEARCH_QUESTION: Does a larger structural Stop buffer improve results?
PRIMARY_CHANGED_VARIABLE: methodology_parameter_bindings.BUFFER_ATR_MULTIPLIER -> 0.40
EXPECTED_INFORMATION_GAIN: Buffer protection and resulting risk geometry.
SELECTION_DISPOSITION: FINAL_SELECTED
DEPENDENCY_RISK: COUPLED
ELIMINATION_OR_RESERVE_REASON: ''
```

## C-040 — Dynamic TP maximum distance

## C-041 — Structural reward-to-risk gate

```yaml
CANDIDATE_ID: C-041
TITLE: Structural reward-to-risk gate
PRIMARY_AXIS: AX-32
HYPOTHESIS_CLASS: POSITION_RULES
PRIMARY_RESEARCH_QUESTION: Does enabling minimum gross R:R=2 improve selection?
PRIMARY_CHANGED_VARIABLE: methodology_supported_fields.minimum_risk_reward.enabled -> True
EXPECTED_INFORMATION_GAIN: Pre-grant gross-geometry gate, not net_rr.
SELECTION_DISPOSITION: FINAL_SELECTED
DEPENDENCY_RISK: COUPLED
ELIMINATION_OR_RESERVE_REASON: ''
BASELINE:
  set: SET-R-001-V1
  position: POS-R-001
  portfolio: PR-R-201
  set_reference_scope: NON_BTC_DEFAULT_ONLY; explicit set_by_symbol is authoritative for execution
  set_by_symbol:
    BTC: SET-R-BTC-001-V1
    ETH: SET-R-001-V1
    SOL: SET-R-001-V1
    XRP: SET-R-001-V1
    DOGE: SET-R-001-V1
    SUI: SET-R-001-V1
    PEPE: SET-R-001-V1
    AVAX: SET-R-001-V1

    BNB: SET-R-001-V1
  symbol_applicability:

      reason: NON_F005_GENERIC_BRANCH_OVER_EXISTING_RETURN_5M

CONTROLLED_VARIABLES: All configuration content outside the named contrast, source/version/cutoff policies, selected
  run interval, data manifests, initial wallet/native-side state, and evidence rules. Endogenous downstream effects
  are not claimed to be held fixed. Research V1 adds the same immutable ten-member universe, source/trace/run/report
  profiles and evidence-retention controls to all comparable arms. All ten allocations are exactly 10%, sum 100%;
  explicit symbol Set bindings, common source/refinement map and RESEARCH_V1_FOCUSED_CORRECTION_R2 are pinned across
  compared arms. BTC qualification context differs by subject, never by treatment except the assigned Set variable.

  remain and ambiguous runs may be unavailable.
```

## C-050 — Coin allocation target and cap

```yaml
CANDIDATE_ID: C-050
TITLE: Coin allocation target and cap
PRIMARY_AXIS: AX-36
HYPOTHESIS_CLASS: PORTFOLIO_RULES
PRIMARY_RESEARCH_QUESTION: Does raising only the ETH allocation target/cap change portfolio results?
PRIMARY_CHANGED_VARIABLE: 'methodology_supported_fields.coins[symbol=ETH].allocation_pct: 20 -> 30'
EXPECTED_INFORMATION_GAIN: Coin budget and tranche grant effects; allocation sums may exceed global cap.
SELECTION_DISPOSITION: INVALID_FOR_RESEARCH_V1_PRODUCT_CONSTRAINT
DEPENDENCY_RISK: COUPLED
ELIMINATION_OR_RESERVE_REASON: 10×10%=100% is fixed; ETH allocation variation is prohibited. Original arm refs below
  are HISTORICAL_ONLY. R-023 revision R2 selects C-036.

  are not claimed to be held fixed. Research V1 adds the same immutable ten-member universe, source/trace/run/report
  profiles and evidence-retention controls to all comparable arms.
```

## C-051 — Global slot pressure

## C-055 — Daily realized-loss latch


## C-056 — Tighter enabled daily loss boundary

```yaml
CANDIDATE_ID: C-056
TITLE: Tighter enabled daily loss boundary
PRIMARY_AXIS: AX-41
HYPOTHESIS_CLASS: PORTFOLIO_RULES
PRIMARY_RESEARCH_QUESTION: Does a 3% rather than 5% enabled daily-loss limit improve results?
PRIMARY_CHANGED_VARIABLE: Tighter enabled daily loss boundary
EXPECTED_INFORMATION_GAIN: Same daily-loss mechanism; reserve.
SELECTION_DISPOSITION: RESERVE
DEPENDENCY_RISK: CONDITIONALLY_INDEPENDENT
ELIMINATION_OR_RESERVE_REASON: ''
BASELINE:
  set: SET-R-001-V1
  position: POS-R-001
  portfolio: PR-R-208
VARIANT:
  set: SET-R-001-V1
  position: POS-R-001
  portfolio: PR-R-209
CONTROLLED_VARIABLES: All configuration content outside the named contrast, source/version/cutoff policies, selected
  run interval, data manifests, initial wallet/native-side state, and evidence rules. Endogenous downstream effects
  are not claimed to be held fixed. Research V1 adds the same immutable ten-member universe, source/trace/run/report
  profiles and evidence-retention controls to all comparable arms.
EXECUTION_FEASIBILITY: Not admitted for Research V1 execution in this focused finalization. Original exclusion/elimination/reserve
  rationale is retained; B-01/B-02 are not the reason. Reserve NO means not execution-certified here, not necessarily
  an invalid source mechanism.
INTERPRETABILITY: High for the primary contrast if the stated prerequisites are met; no empirical independence assertion.
WEB_RESEARCH_EXECUTABILITY: 'NO'
OVERLAP_RISK: Evaluated for every pair in artifact C; baseline infrastructure overlap alone is not duplicate information.
```
